# Experiment manifest — Stage A reuse map

Snapshot: 2026-09-04. Generated by `analysis/v22_experiment_manifest.py` from local artifacts. This catalogs completed measurements and derived results, including gitignored weights/caches. No model inference, training, scheduler access, or checkpoint deserialization was performed.

Regenerate: `python analysis/v22_experiment_manifest.py`; list inputs without writes: `python analysis/v22_experiment_manifest.py --dry-run`. Run V22 decomposition separately with `python analysis/v22_distill_decomp.py --dry-run` / `--update-law-report`.

Status tags: **HAVE** = existing derived result/report; **REUSABLE** = existing measurement or supporting artifact to reuse; **TO-ANALYZE** = existing evidence needs the named audit/refit; **TO-MEASURE** = absent required measurement, not authorization to run it; **RUNNING** = affirmatively recorded active run. No RUNNING status is asserted here: file presence/absence does not establish job state, and running jobs were not inspected. Reusable does not imply scientific validation or verified binary checkpoint completeness.

All primary loss cells below have math/code/QA unless explicitly noted. L is completion-token weighted CE nats per model-token, and ΔL uses that arm's own dense anchor except explicitly source-referenced distillation. Training D is examples **per domain**; recovery D_R counts supervised causal target tokens. Density and b/16 are nominal compression coordinates, not measured storage or latency. See [MEASUREMENT_AUDIT.md](MEASUREMENT_AUDIT.md).

## Available family size panels

| Family | Nominal sizes available | Pruning / quantization | Distilled students present |
|---|---|---|---|
| gemma3 | 270m / 1b / 4b / 12b / 27b | all five, dense + ladders | 270m / 1b / 4b / 12b |
| qwen3 | 0.6b / 1.7b / 4b | all three, dense + ladders | 0.6b / 1.7b |
| olmo3 | 7b / 32b | both, dense + ladders | 7b only |
| gemma4 | 31b | dense + ladders | none |
| muse | 30b | dense + ladders | none |

These are available checkpoint panels, not a complete cross-product of every arm/config. The inventory keeps five named families; V18 uses a different four-family grouping. Qwen V10/V9 tags `Qwen--Qwen3-*` alias V6/V12 `Qwen3-*`. Exact registry IDs below are recorded metadata, without immutable revision hashes.

Later response-only panels may lack `fisher_meta.json`; their checkpoint ID and weight count are marked not recorded rather than inferred from a directory name. All files remain indexed. Metrics/artifacts per model: `prune_losses.json` (L and derived ΔL), `alignment.json` (signed first-order / second-order diagnostics), `spectrum_bins.npz` and `fisher_meta.json` (binned Fisher/magnitude spectra and weight scope), `probes.json` (counts, not frozen item IDs), `report.md` / `report_b.md`. Available raw alignment/spectrum caches are individually indexed below. Preserve infill densities: Gemma 1B has six extra points; Qwen 1.7B has five. `_infill_meta` is metadata, not a density. **TO-ANALYZE:** provenance-limited `Qwen3-0.6B/archive-v1-sameprobe/` is an older same-probe variant; exclude it from the current disjoint Fisher/measurement analysis.

All 12 models already have dense plus int8/int6/int4/int3. Existing `report.md` siblings are HAVE. The final scan additionally includes separate Qwen 0.6B/1.7B int5 artifacts under `Qwen3-*` tags. These use their own newly recorded dense anchors: do not merge aliases by overwriting anchors or subtracting a different run's dense loss. The `results/prereg/quant_5bit_prereg.json` predictions are a separate derived artifact; they are not measurements. No packed-weight size, memory, or latency measurement is implied.

Do not infer a completed 64M cell. All other recorded ladders contain three completed budgets.

Loss also changes with the selected panel. These are separate eval versions even though both serialize `version=15`. V19 uses only seven models × {dense,.8,.7,.6}, 28 easy checkpoints; deeper .5 and three distilled easy cells remain available for separate analysis.

| Scope | Status | Action / evidence |

| 5-bit forecast scoring | TO-ANALYZE | Existing prereg JSON and separate Qwen .6/1.7B int5 results; verify frozen provenance and use each measurement's own dense anchor. |
| Frozen checkpoint-group split manifest | TO-ANALYZE | Build immutable fold assignments from existing cells; shallow density and D holdouts are config holdouts on reused checkpoints. |
| V15 legacy/default vs easy and V12/V16 discrepancies | TO-ANALYZE | Keep separate protocols; audit checkpoint/tokenizer revisions; existing evaluations need no repeat for current decomposition. |
| Cross-tokenizer common-text loss | TO-MEASURE | Per-byte/per-character NLL on frozen common prompt/target spans is absent; future measurement only. |
| C21 independent transfer evidence | TO-MEASURE | Additional Qwen sizes/references/families/seeds with matched training mode and frozen grouping; two-size saturated slope has no held-out test. |
| Missing V16 Qwen generic controls | TO-MEASURE | Neither .6B nor1.7B has residual.json; V12 own-dense gains already exist. |
| Recovery generalization | TO-MEASURE | Denser budgets / independent seeds and checkpoint groups; reuse all eight completed ladders. |
| Cost axes | TO-MEASURE | Serialized storage, runtime memory, latency are not measured by nominal density/bits/parameter counts. |

No absent cell is labeled failed or running. These gaps are documentation only; Stage A does not launch them.
